8                                                                                                              8

9    Each original rational objective, trained-parameter payload, full exhaustive reference, adaptive answer   9

10   and clock is retained. The additional action and optional second shock are explicit nested model          10

11   extensions; the original scalar-action economy is recovered by zero second action with the second shock   11

12   disabled.                                                                                                 12

13                                                                                                             13

14     For independent box-uniform components, repeated integration of the positive                            14

15   part gives the exact signed vertex formula in the main article. Zero radii are removed                    15

16   before division. A finite mixture is evaluated with nonnegative rational mixture                          16

17   weights. Signed network output weights reverse interval endpoints when necessary. 17
18   This operation is not an assumption that the sum of two uniforms is itself uniform. 18
19     The two-control objective retains both nonlinear investment costs, the stated                           19

20   cross term, both exposure columns and the shared capacity constraint. The original                        20

21   scalar action-dependent objective is recovered on the stated nested restriction. Every                    21

22   feasible lattice pair is evaluated by the exhaustive reference. Subdivision starts from                   22

23   a feasible exact incumbent, prunes only with a valid lower bound and lexicographic                        23

24   tie test, and can fall back to the same exhaustive problem. The second-action and                         24

25   second-shock query records are not relabeled as full policy sweeps or as a calibrated                     25

26   economic experiment.                                                                                      26

27                                                                                                             27
                 58. ORIGINAL-OPTIMUM BRACKETS AND FIXED-POLICY REVALIDATION
28                                                                                                             28

29     The common-reference construction keeps Jt∗ − Jt0 and Jtπ − Jt0 on the same state. 29
30   Its narrower bracket is not obtained by changing the initial law, replacing continu- 30
31   ous feasible actions by a finite comparison menu, or dropping the cost of a future                        31

32   actor. Complete continuous-action interval covers supply the Bellman lower bound. 32
                          NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: SUPPLEMENT                          163

1    Only robustly feasible quantum actions construct its upper policy. Statewise resid- 1
2    ual differences and original-law innovation bins provide the signed continuation          2

3    calculation without a policy-value oracle.                                                3

4      The refinement grid ( N, A, q) follows the sequence                                     4

5                                                                                              5

6                             (8, 8, 4),   (16, 16, 8),   (32, 32, 16),                        6

7                                  (64, 64, 32),   (128, 64, 32).                              7

8                                                                                              8

9    The final action quantum stays fixed, and the last action-cover count is held at          9

10   64. The measured ladder therefore has a stated residual tolerance, not a claim of         10

11   an executed arbitrary-accuracy limit. The consistency theorem separately requires         11

12   uniformly vanishing local approximation and arithmetic allowances and stable              12

13   endpoint maps, including refinement of a binding action quantum when needed.              13

14     The new fixed-policy calculation includes all thirty-two original two-date service      14

15   aliases. Reconstruction of the full policy-plus-partition identity yields five distinct   15

16   actors; equality of one expected cost is not used for deduplication. The verification     16

17   grid is fixed before these actor bounds are calculated. Every fine cell includes all      17

18   intersecting acquired leaves and both closed endpoints. No smoothness hypothesis          18

19   is imposed on a discontinuous actor, and no midpoint action is silently assigned to       19

20   a cell crossed by an actor boundary.                                                      20

21     The nonpositive reference-advantage gate is independently reconstructed at              21

22   every original acquired cell and date before the zero-excess bound is used. The           22

23   upper recursion and its unclipped counterpart are then both recomputed from the           23

24   primitives, and both endpoint arrays are retained. Their maximum all-date gaps            24

25   happen to coincide in this catalogue; that is an observed result, not a reason to omit    25

26   the unclipped calculation. Original policies, seeds, partitions, stopping targets and     26

27   inference samples are unchanged.                                                          27

28     Each actor’s additional cost covers the complete upper recursion and endpoint           28

29   archive. A common lower-table reconstruction is shared verification work. The outer       29

30   process record includes imports, the log and its first durable receipt. These clocks      30

31   refer to their actual executions. The original centered ladder’s construction time,       31

32   a later reconstruction time and an earlier prospective service on another worker          32
     164

1    are not summed and called one observed end-to-end run. Such components can               1

2    be separately budgeted for a future deployment, but that is a proposed allocation        2

3    rather than a measured historical service.                                               3

4                                                                                             4

5                            59. PUBLICATION AND PRESERVATION                                 5

6                                                                                             6
       The publication script uses ordinary committed manuscript sources. It verifies
7                                                                                             7
     the predecessor preservation maps and all new source freezes, reruns the exact
8                                                                                             8
     theorem and implementation regressions, checks every inherited labeled result,
9                                                                                             9
     regenerates the current tables and compiles the seven-document package. A clean
10                                                                                            10
     archive rebuild removes generated PDFs and current tables before repeating the
11                                                                                            11
     ordinary build. Output page counts and extracted-text identities are compared, and
12                                                                                            12
     every delivered source, evidence file and PDF has a final identity record.
13                                                                                            13
       New proof statements and the fixed-cover consistency clarification are distin-
14                                                                                            14
     guished from inherited results. The original R59 article and introduction files remain
15                                                                                            15
     preserved, as do all original economic applications and adverse numerical com-
16                                                                                            16
     parisons in the complete development companions. The active introduction now
17                                                                                            17
     supplies a single reading path rather than repeated revision prefaces. The title and
18                                                                                            18
     economic research subject remain Neural Bellman Operators. Compilation, source
19                                                                                            19
     identity, exact regression tests and deterministic reproduction are evidence about
20                                                                                            20
     the delivered calculation, not an external mathematical acceptance decision.
21                                                                                            21

      60. ORIGINAL-OPTIMUM VERIFICATION: IMPLEMENTATION AND COMPLETE RECORDS
23                                                                                            23

24     The main article proves primitive regularity and the whole-domain actual-policy        24

25   certificate. This supplement describes the precise numerical object to which those       25

26   results are applied and reports the entire fixed catalogue. The discount is the          26

27   inherited 15/16 in the theorem, training objectives, path costs and all recursions. 27
28   A compatibility fixture checks that the new one-date original-optimum bracket            28

29   contains the independently implemented original terminal-action objective. That          29

30   fixture caught a draft discount mismatch before the production source freeze; the        30

31   correction and failed fixture are preserved, not counted as a failed economic design     31

32   or hidden as a successful production run.                                                32
                          NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: SUPPLEMENT                          165

1                        60.1. Positive interpolation and acquired actions                     1

2                                                                                              2

3                                                                                              3

4      The state mesh has (n + 1)d vertices. Indices use row-major order, and a point on       4

5    the upper face is owned by the last adjacent interpolation cell. All tensor weights are   5

6    nonnegative. In the native kernel, multiplication by the power-of-two subdivision         6

7    count and subtraction of the integer cell index precede the explicit tensor products. 7
8    The binary64 format, nearest rounding mode, finite input arrays and state-domain          8

9    membership are checked. The kernel is built without reassociation or floating- 9
10   point contraction; neither a BLAS sum nor an unverified optimizer supplies the            10

11   interpolated endpoint.                                                                    11

12     The arithmetic allowance expands each stored endpoint by the proved absolute            12

13   summation bound. To enclose a transition rectangle, the algorithm evaluates its           13

14   midpoint and adds the sum of its coordinate radii multiplied by the table’s recorded      14

15   Lipschitz constants. Those constants are obtained from outward adjacent differences. 15
16   This enclosure applies to the possibly nonconvex numerical endpoint table; the            16

17   separate second-order interpolation allowance concerns the true convex Bellman            17

18   value. Confusing those two statements would omit a necessary error.                       18

19     The actual policy first rounds a true state downward at forty bits, evaluates the       19

20   positive interpolation of nodal actions at that acquired state, and rounds a lower        20

21   numerical endpoint downward at twenty action bits. Nonnegative truncation re- 21
22   moves a spurious negative endpoint at zero investment. Feasibility follows because        22

23   each nodal vector is in the capacity simplex, capacity is affine, and downward            23

24   state acquisition cannot increase capacity. The action error account includes state       24

25   acquisition, the arithmetic lower expansion and action rounding. The path evaluator       25

26   encloses this implemented rule, rather than evaluating an ideal infinite-precision        26

27   actor and assigning its cost to a different controller.                                   27

28     The pure fitted nodal action is obtained by transferring the coarse exact or ap- 28
29   proximate proposal as a fraction of capacity, followed by downward quantum                29

30   rounding and an exact dyadic capacity check. This transfer can change the original        30

31   fitted-lattice optimum. The new theorem certifies the action actually transferred; it     31

32   does not claim that this fine-grid nodal action remains the exact minimizer of the        32
     166

1    stored critic. Likewise, interpolation changes the old cell-constant actor. The earlier   1

2    unchanged-actor revalidation remains a separate experiment with its own results.          2

3                                                                                              3

4                    60.2. Separate continuous-action and innovation errors                    4

5
       A lower Bellman bound covers the entire continuous capacity interval or simplex. 5
6                                                                                              6
     It subtracts the second-order lattice allowance even when the best proposed nodal
7                                                                                              7
     action is found exactly. The upper endpoint needs only a feasible action. The
8                                                                                              8
     terminal function is evaluated directly on each transition enclosure, so no terminal
9                                                                                              9
     state-interpolation allowance is charged in that operation.
10                                                                                             10
       For each innovation bin, the midpoint is its conditional mean under the original
11                                                                                             11
     uniform law. Jensen’s inequality and the proved semiconcavity supply two different
12                                                                                             12
     directions of approximation. The lower calculation uses the former; the upper
13                                                                                             13
     adds the conditional-variance allowance. With two independent components, the
14                                                                                             14
     variances add and every Cartesian bin is included. The proof concerns the true
15                                                                                             15
     Bellman continuation. No convexity of a fitted ReLU or of an arbitrary numerical
16                                                                                             16
     endpoint array is assumed.
17                                                                                             17
       The nodal original-optimum bracket is not itself the complete policy bound. The
18                                                                                             18
     latter adds the off-grid optimal-advantage allowance and the future policy error at
19                                                                                             19
     every date. All lower arrays, selected-action upper arrays, exact rational constants,
20                                                                                             20
     deployment allowances and recursions are deposited for every method, seed and
21                                                                                             21
     rung. The common-augmented selector minimizes a certified upper endpoint for
22                                                                                             22
     the original-optimum continuation. This is not the earlier directed incumbent-policy
23                                                                                             23
     improvement gate: a tighter certificate does not establish statewise cost dominance
24                                                                                             24
     over the common policy. Direct policy-cost contrasts are therefore retained.
25                                                                                             25

       R and Q denote trained ReLU and fitted quadratic proposals. A fraction is an exact count over the

     four declared seeds; it is not a population probability. The common comparator has no training seed.
25                                                                                                            25
     Every unsuccessful target remains budget exhausted. Exact first-rung identities and the least recorded
26   full-release work among eligible methods are retained in the machine-readable publication facts.         26

1      Both variants and every rung are retained. Upper-bound ranges cover every fixed seed. Complete       1

2    nodal arrays, exact error recursions, interpolation and quadrature allowances, action discretization   2

3    allowances, and deployment errors are retained for each entry.                                         3

4                                                                                                           4
                  61. COMPLETE POLICY COSTS AND FINITE ECONOMIC DECISIONS
5                                                                                                           5

6      The fixed initial distribution is uniform on the full state cube. The first shock                    6

7    has alternating exposure and radius 1/32; the two-control extension adds the                           7

8    independent common-direction shock of radius 1/64. Forty-bit bin integers define                       8

9    outward enclosures of all continuous initial and innovation realizations. Every                        9

10   running cost and the terminal cost are enclosed along the entire acquired policy                       10

11   path. The path arrays retain negative paired gains when they occur.                                    11

12     A valid independent support is 103/64 for each nonnegative running cost and                          12

13   37/16 for terminal cost. The exact discounted support is                                               13

14                                                                                                          14
                                     103 T −1           37
15                              HT =     ∑
                                      64 t=0
                                              (15/16)t + (15/16)T .
                                                        16                                                  15

16                                                                                                          16

17   The support of a signed paired difference is [− HT , HT ]. It is not reduced to a                      17

18   nonnegative support by appealing to a claim about expected improvement. The                            18

19   bounded empirical-Bernstein calculation uses the same retained moment engine                           19

20   as the preceding study, with a conservative logarithmic upper bound sixteen. The                       20

21   union account covers at most 512 declared interval objects with family error 1/100;                    21

22   no independence between policies or estimands is required for that union bound. 22
23   The sampling model concerns independent bin rows, not a theorem that a fixed                           23

24   pseudorandom seed is intrinsically random.                                                             24

25     Policy arrays with identical bytes on the same nodal grid define identical acquired                  25

26   actors. Their contrasts are exactly zero and their path evaluation is deduplicated. 26
27   Distinct fitting seeds, common path coupling and deduplication are all recorded                        27

28   explicitly. Selecting a returned controller or an installation-fee interval from this                  28

29   frozen finite family is covered by the same simultaneous event. It does not extend                     29

30   coverage to a newly trained policy or an undeclared initial law.                                       30

26                                                                                                       26
                        62. COMBINED RESOURCE AND RELIABILITY ACCOUNT
27                                                                                                       27

28     Closing the measured production boundary. The frozen per-method release field                     28

29   sums measured core components. The publication audit found that common- 29
30   certificate bookkeeping, some output records and the final actor-file serialization                 30

31   were outside those individual clocks. We preserve the field unchanged as a compo- 31
32   nent sum, rather than calling it an isolated complete runtime. The recorded outer                   32
     174

1    production step spans 466 seconds at whole-second timestamp resolution; a trans- 1
2    parent two-second reserve gives a 468-second accounting envelope. Subtracting            2

3    the nonoverlapping component clocks leaves a shared unallocated residual. The            3

4    table charges that entire residual to every method, in addition to its own component     4

5    sum. These are conservative complete-production charges, not separately observed         5

6    method runtimes or statistical timing confidence bounds. Remote publication, doc- 6
7    ument production and replay have their own receipts. This correction changes             7

8    neither a scientific result nor a measured service clock.                                8

9      Let w denote the number of scalar ridge and squared-hinge terms, L the feasi- 9
10   ble action-lattice cardinality, and Bc the input coefficient bit length. Combining       10

11   the retained root-free witness bound with the new verification account gives the         11

12   following conservative conditional work decomposition:                                   12

13                                                                                            13

14
             Wcomplete ≤ Wfit + Wtransfer + Wserialize + Winference + Wcompile                14
                                                                              
15                       + O TL0 {w2 + w log(w + 1) + w log( L + 1)}Crat (b)                  15

16                                                                                          16

17
                         + O T ( n + 1 ) d K A q s 2d d ,                                     17

18                                                                                            18
     where L0 is the coarse proposal-leaf count and
19                                                                                            19

20             b = O((w + 1){ Bc + log( Q + 1) + log( L + 1) + log(w + 2)}) .                 20

21                                                                                            21

22   The scalar exact-search term applies only to the retained rational piecewise-quartic     22

23   subclass. For several controls it is replaced by the actual constrained proposal cost,   23

24   or by the product-lattice and shock-vertex costs of the exact retained extension         24

25   when that extension is used. The new two-control production proposal uses SLSQP,         25

26   not an unexecuted claim that exact subdivision has become fast.                          26

27     For fixed dimension and horizon, the analytic state, action and innovation al- 27
28   lowances are respectively of order n−2 , A−2 and q−2 , plus the explicit deployment      28

29   and arithmetic terms. Their constants are the stated Bellman regularity constants. 29
30   Choosing each resolution sufficiently large therefore makes the original-optimum         30

31   policy bound small for the common construction and its augmented policies. A             31

32   pure fitted policy has the additional recorded proposal-loss term, which does not        32
                              NEURAL BELLMAN OPERATORS: SUPPLEMENT                                        175

1    vanish simply by refining the verification mesh. The factors (n + 1)d , K A , qs and 2d                      1

2    remain in the computational account.                                                                         2

3      The native rational solver’s stored diagnostics describe evaluated operands,                               3

4    candidate counts, exact sign queries, and serialized requests and responses. They                            4

5    do not measure every temporary denominator or internal normalization allocation. 5
6    Similarly, the explicit interpolation-array lengths do not measure total bytes moved                         6

7    through hardware memory. Those unmeasured quantities are not assigned zero. 7
8    The release records process peak resident memory for the full science process, not                           8

9    an independent peak-memory observation for each generator.                                                   9

10     The finite four-seed catalogue permits an exact attained fraction within each                              10

11   declared task. It supplies no confidence interval for optimizer reliability on an un- 11
12   specified population of economic models. Every source freeze precedes production,                            12

13   and every unsuccessful target remains in the record. The publication build replays                           13

14   arrays and inference arithmetic without fitting, drawing new paths, or replacing                             14

15   scientific service clocks.                                                                                   15
